# Residential decor table

Generated by `unity command gangsters_decor_table` from the direct nested-prefab children of the fourteen harvested residential units. Every XYZ/yaw tuple below is an observed transform rounded to 0.001 in its member anchor frame; the generator never uses a centroid or averaged transform.

Rows with the same `variant` are one atomic measured template. `part/parts` fixes member order; signed column/row/floor offsets select each member's host on the measured 5 m by 5 m by 3 m grid. XYZ/yaw then remain local to that actual host piece pivot/yaw. Host kind/style prevent reuse on an incompatible shell module. Fire-escape role 1/2/3 is bottom/middle/top; only the observed middle member has `repeat=yes`, so it may repeat unchanged on intermediate target floors. Min/mean/max are per-variant rates. Family min/mean/max sum unique templates once per building for the exact family+anchor+relation bucket, divide by that building's eligible anchors, and include all fourteen zero buildings; every member row repeats the same bucket rate.

Every ignored or geometrically unbound occurrence remains under Unresolved. Readiness is gated per supported family at no more than 20% unbound; ignored brownstone, environment and unsupported loose street dressing never enters a usable rule.
A washing-line relation is accepted only when both ends of its measured five-metre local-X mesh meet the actual measured fire-escape attachment roots of two host columns that are adjacent on the facade grid.

Style votes by facade column 01/02/03: **65/63/49**. Fire-escape column share: **0.25–1**. Captured templates: **731**.
Fire-escape eligibility matches the forge's interior long-row columns: ApartmentCorner hosts are excluded from both numerator and denominator; **60** such source occurrences were named as ignored-out-of-target.
Families not ready for generation: **WashingLine**. A not-ready family remains measured and named below, but is omitted by the forge.
